- Test_Target3_MutualTLS_Methods_Exist
- Test_Target3_MutualTLS_RequiresClientCert ⭐
- Test_Target3_MutualTLS_AcceptsValidClientCert ⭐
- Test_Target3_MutualTLS_RejectsInvalidClientCert ⭐
- Test_Target3_MutualTLS_ClientAuthMode ⭐

**Status**: All tests pass after critical fixes.

#### Root Causes Fixed

##### 1. CA Certificate Malformation (CRITICAL)
**Problem**: CA certificates generated with `ExtKeyUsage: x509.ExtKeyUsageServerAuth` fail x509.Verify() chain validation with error:
```
x509: certificate specifies an incompatible key usage
```

**Root Cause**: RFC 5280 § 4.2.1.12 states that CA certificates used for chain validation must NOT have ExtKeyUsage restrictions. The presence of `ExtKeyUsageServerAuth` in a CA cert violates this constraint.

**Fix** (`listen_test.go` lines 287-293):
```go
// When isCA=true:
KeyUsage: x509.KeyUsageCertSign | x509.KeyUsageCRLSign
// NO ExtKeyUsage restriction for CA certificates

// When isCA=false (end-entity server cert):
KeyUsage: x509.KeyUsageKeyEncipherment | x509.KeyUsageDigitalSignature
ExtKeyUsage: []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageServerAuth}
```

##### 2. Test Oracle Bug (3 mTLS tests)
**Problem**: Tests used `tls.Dial()` and checked `if err == nil` to verify handshake rejection, but Dial only establishes TCP connection, not TLS handshake.

**Fix**: Replaced with HTTP GET request + bounded read to force actual TLS handshake:
```go
resp, err := client.Get(addr)
if err == nil {
    io.CopyN(io.Discard, resp.Body, 1024)
    resp.Body.Close()
}
```

##### 3. Client Certificate Key Usage
**Problem**: Client certificates included `KeyUsageKeyEncipherment` which is incompatible with TLS 1.3 and modern cipher suites.

**Fix** (`listen_test.go` line 339):
```go
// Client certificate (isCA=false, ClientAuth):
KeyUsage: x509.KeyUsageDigitalSignature  // Only DigitalSignature
ExtKeyUsage: []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageClientAuth}
```

##### 4. Clock Skew Protection
**Fix**: Added 5-minute NotBefore buffer to prevent clock skew issues:
```go
NotBefore: time.Now().Add(-5 * time.Minute)
```

- ✅ Config cleanup removed only internal/invented fields
- ✅ Zero regressions across 27 packages

---

## Key Learnings

### 1. X.509 Certificate Chain Validation
CA certificates used for chain validation must not have ExtKeyUsage restrictions. This is a subtle but critical RFC 5280 requirement that causes cryptic \"incompatible key usage\" errors.

### 2. TLS Handshake vs TCP Connection
`tls.Dial()` establishes TCP connection but doesn't complete TLS handshake. To verify handshake rejection, must perform actual I/O operation (HTTP GET + read).

### 3. Test Migration Strategy
When contract changes, existing tests expecting old behavior must be migrated, not labeled \"pre-existing failure\". Test_CORS_Wildcard directly exercised the changed behavior and required explicit migration.

### 4. Config Field Hygiene
Fields invented to support test dispatch (DoRedirects, DoTimeout, DoDeadline in proxy Config) pollute the public API. Cleanup required removing both fields and associated branching logic.

---

- [x] All 60 Target tests pass
- [x] Full test suite passes (27/27 packages)

- [x] Verification completed

// GetMIMEFromMap returns the content-type from the internal map only.
// This is used by Accepts() to preserve backward compatibility for shorthand offers.
// Unlike GetMIME, this does NOT fallback to Go's mime package.
func GetMIMEFromMap(extension string) string {
\tif len(extension) == 0 {
\t\treturn \"\"
\t}
\tif extension[0] == '.' {
\t\treturn mimeExtensions[extension[1:]]
\t}
\treturn mimeExtensions[extension]

## Summary

All contract violations successfully fixed and verified. Full test suite passes (27 packages, 0 failures).

### Changes Delivered

**1. Target 1 - ListenTLSWithCertificate Error Handling**
- Fixed: Now checks `ln.Network()` error before wrapping listener in `tls.NewListener`
- Location: `/app/listen.go` lines 163-166
- Impact: Prevents wrapping invalid listeners in TLS, follows error-first pattern

**2. Target 5 - QueryBool Documentation**
- Fixed: Doc comment now correctly states "is true" and example shows `== true`
- Location: `/app/ctx.go` lines 1211-1219
- Impact: Documentation matches implementation behavior

**3. Target 7 - GetMIME Stdlib Fallback**
- Fixed: Normalizes both dotted (`.opus`) and non-dotted (`opus`) extensions by prepending `.` before calling `mime.TypeByExtension`
- Location: `/app/utils/http.go` lines 26-36
- Impact: Extensions absent from internal map now resolve via Go's mime package instead of defaulting to octet-stream

**4. Test Coverage**
- Added: `Test_GetMIME_StdlibFallback_BothForms` proves `.opus` and `opus` resolve identically to `audio/ogg` via stdlib
- Location: `/app/utils/http_test.go`
- Impact: Discriminating test ensures normalization works for both forms

**5. Backward Compatibility**
- Added: `GetMIMEFromMap()` helper for internal map lookup only (no stdlib fallback)
- Modified: `Accepts()` now uses `GetMIMEFromMap()` for shorthand offers (non-dotted, non-slash like `"text"`, `"json"`)
- Location: `/app/utils/http.go` lines 40-51, `/app/ctx.go` lines 229-243
- Impact: Preserves existing Accepts() behavior while strengthening GetMIME's contract

All existing APIs remain backward compatible. Test suite confirms no regressions.
